        Idea:
Finish the projection-model rollback that a previous megaplan started but only got ~60% done. Currently the data shape is soft-tag (pinnedShotGroups[i] = { shotId, trackId, clipIds }) but several production code paths still operate on the legacy projection-model fields (start, children, offset, duration) behind compatibility shims that are silently lossy. The result: every interaction with a shot group goes through a hybrid where live preview uses soft-tag overrides but commit goes through legacy materialize paths, causing rounding drift, position jumps, stuck preview frames after release, and pointer-capture-loss races.

This is a NARROW, SCOPED finish-the-job megaplan, not a re-architecture. We know exactly what's left.

State of partial completion:

DONE (don't touch unless broken):
- PinnedShotGroup type is soft-tag in src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (with deprecated start? and children? legacy compat fields that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- pinned-group-projection.ts collapsed to ~280 lines: findEnclosingPinnedGroup, categorizeSelection, orderClipIdsByAt (real) + buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups (deprecated compatibility shims that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- useClipDrag.ts rewritten for soft-tag model: uses enclosingGroup.group.clipIds, computes group bounds from live actions, uses orderClipIdsByAt to re-derive clip order after drags
- useClipResize.ts rewritten with resolveGroupMembers, getInteriorNeighbor, computeClipResizeMetaPatch, applyActionBounds helpers — interior-boundary commit branch is in place but routes through applyEdit({type: 'rows'}) which goes through the lossy materializeData path
- useShotGroups.ts rewritten to derive children/start from live row actions
- interaction-state.ts shared interaction lib created with createInteractionState, isInteractionActive, onInteractionEnd, notifyInteractionEndIfIdle
- useTimelinePersistence.ts save scheduler IS gated on interactionStateRef and flushes on gesture end
- usePollSync.ts poll-acceptance gate IS gated on interactionStateRef and re-evaluates on gesture end
- interactionStateRef is plumbed through VideoEditorProvider data context
- TimelineCanvas resize session model collapsed to one discriminated union: 'clip' | 'group-child-boundary' | 'group-edge' with one handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, handleResizePointerMove

NOT DONE (this megaplan):

1. useClipResize.ts interior-boundary commit drift. The interior commit calls applyEdit({type: 'rows', rows: nextRows, metaUpdates}). applyEdit for 'rows' mutations routes through materializeData -> rowsToConfig -> assembleTimelineData -> configToRows. rowsToConfig rounds clip positions to 2 decimal places (0.01s precision) and serializes hold clips as (at, hold) rounded INDEPENDENTLY, which means derived_end != round(actual_end) and tiny gaps can open between adjacent clips after every commit. Fix by increasing rowsToConfig rounding precision to at least 4 decimal places, OR better, make hold clip at/end serialization consistent.

2. usePinnedGroupSync rewrite for soft-tag model in src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts. Currently iterates projection-model children/offset/duration arrays. Rewrite to watch underlying Shot.images[] for added/removed/reordered generation_ids and rebuild clipIds accordingly, deferring during interaction.

3. useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts rewrite for soft-tag model. Currently builds projection-shape nextChildren arrays and passes to buildGroupFromChildren/replaceGroupChildren shims. Rewrite to compute the final-video clip's at/hold directly, update pinnedShotGroups[i].clipIds to [finalVideoClipId], save image clip positions in imageClipSnapshot. Restore reads imageClipSnapshot and restores row actions with updated clipIds.

4. useExternalDrop.ts rewrite for soft-tag shot drop. Compute clip positions directly when adding row actions, create soft-tag group with clipIds.

5. useTimelineTrackManagement.ts moveGroup callsites. Currently uses moveGroup shim which only updates trackId. Rewrite three callsites to also re-derive each clip's row assignment in meta.

6. Migration in migrate.ts repairConfig. Add one-way migration: when loading a saved config with projection-shape groups, convert to soft-tag by extracting clipIds = children.map((c) => c.clipId) and dropping start/children. Add unit test.

7. Delete deprecated compatibility shims from pinned-group-projection.ts: buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups. Also delete from PinnedShotGroup type: deprecated start? and children? fields. Verify type-check clean.

8. Update or delete the 14 failing tests in: serialize.test.ts (1), timeline-save-utils.test.ts (3), useClipDrag.test.tsx (3), useClipEditing.test.ts (6), useShotGroups.test.ts (1). Rewrite to test soft-tag model or delete if invariant no longer makes sense.

9. Remove diagnostic console.logs for tags: [handleResizePointerDown], [outer-edge lookup], [GroupEdgeResize], [startGroupEdgeResize], [useClipDrag], [Marquee].

Hard requirements:
1. Two user-reported symptoms must be fixed: boundary jumps closer to adjacent clip / cuts into other side; doesn't release frames after release.
2. All listed flows must continue to work end-to-end (drag clip in group, drag edges, interior boundary, cross-track, switch to final video, switch to images, gallery drop, auto-save defer, poll-sync defer, projection-shape load migration, shot label menu, per-clip context menu lockdown).
3. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
4. All video-editor tests pass.
5. The 5 deprecated shims and 2 legacy fields physically deleted.
6. No diagnostic console.logs left.

Constraints: scope is video-editor tool only. Follow CLAUDE.md. Prefer Edit over Write. Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Pre-existing failing tests and TS errors in unrelated files left alone. Don't introduce new state management libraries.

Success criteria:

2. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.

6. Loading saved file with projection-shape pinnedShotGroups works (migration test passes).

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Finish the projection-model rollback that a previous megaplan started but only got ~60% done. Currently the data shape is soft-tag (pinnedShotGroups[i] = { shotId, trackId, clipIds }) but several production code paths still operate on the legacy projection-model fields (start, children, offset, duration) behind compatibility shims that are silently lossy. The result: every interaction with a shot group goes through a hybrid where live preview uses soft-tag overrides but commit goes through legacy materialize paths, causing rounding drift, position jumps, stuck preview frames after release, and pointer-capture-loss races.

This is a NARROW, SCOPED finish-the-job megaplan, not a re-architecture. We know exactly what's left.

State of partial completion:

DONE (don't touch unless broken):
- PinnedShotGroup type is soft-tag in src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (with deprecated start? and children? legacy compat fields that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- pinned-group-projection.ts collapsed to ~280 lines: findEnclosingPinnedGroup, categorizeSelection, orderClipIdsByAt (real) + buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups (deprecated compatibility shims that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- useClipDrag.ts rewritten for soft-tag model: uses enclosingGroup.group.clipIds, computes group bounds from live actions, uses orderClipIdsByAt to re-derive clip order after drags
- useClipResize.ts rewritten with resolveGroupMembers, getInteriorNeighbor, computeClipResizeMetaPatch, applyActionBounds helpers — interior-boundary commit branch is in place but routes through applyEdit({type: 'rows'}) which goes through the lossy materializeData path
- useShotGroups.ts rewritten to derive children/start from live row actions
- interaction-state.ts shared interaction lib created with createInteractionState, isInteractionActive, onInteractionEnd, notifyInteractionEndIfIdle
- useTimelinePersistence.ts save scheduler IS gated on interactionStateRef and flushes on gesture end
- usePollSync.ts poll-acceptance gate IS gated on interactionStateRef and re-evaluates on gesture end
- interactionStateRef is plumbed through VideoEditorProvider data context
- TimelineCanvas resize session model collapsed to one discriminated union: 'clip' | 'group-child-boundary' | 'group-edge' with one handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, handleResizePointerMove

NOT DONE (this megaplan):

1. useClipResize.ts interior-boundary commit drift. The interior commit calls applyEdit({type: 'rows', rows: nextRows, metaUpdates}). applyEdit for 'rows' mutations routes through materializeData -> rowsToConfig -> assembleTimelineData -> configToRows. rowsToConfig rounds clip positions to 2 decimal places (0.01s precision) and serializes hold clips as (at, hold) rounded INDEPENDENTLY, which means derived_end != round(actual_end) and tiny gaps can open between adjacent clips after every commit. Fix by increasing rowsToConfig rounding precision to at least 4 decimal places, OR better, make hold clip at/end serialization consistent.

2. usePinnedGroupSync rewrite for soft-tag model in src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts. Currently iterates projection-model children/offset/duration arrays. Rewrite to watch underlying Shot.images[] for added/removed/reordered generation_ids and rebuild clipIds accordingly, deferring during interaction.

3. useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts rewrite for soft-tag model. Currently builds projection-shape nextChildren arrays and passes to buildGroupFromChildren/replaceGroupChildren shims. Rewrite to compute the final-video clip's at/hold directly, update pinnedShotGroups[i].clipIds to [finalVideoClipId], save image clip positions in imageClipSnapshot. Restore reads imageClipSnapshot and restores row actions with updated clipIds.

4. useExternalDrop.ts rewrite for soft-tag shot drop. Compute clip positions directly when adding row actions, create soft-tag group with clipIds.

5. useTimelineTrackManagement.ts moveGroup callsites. Currently uses moveGroup shim which only updates trackId. Rewrite three callsites to also re-derive each clip's row assignment in meta.

6. Migration in migrate.ts repairConfig. Add one-way migration: when loading a saved config with projection-shape groups, convert to soft-tag by extracting clipIds = children.map((c) => c.clipId) and dropping start/children. Add unit test.

7. Delete deprecated compatibility shims from pinned-group-projection.ts: buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups. Also delete from PinnedShotGroup type: deprecated start? and children? fields. Verify type-check clean.

8. Update or delete the 14 failing tests in: serialize.test.ts (1), timeline-save-utils.test.ts (3), useClipDrag.test.tsx (3), useClipEditing.test.ts (6), useShotGroups.test.ts (1). Rewrite to test soft-tag model or delete if invariant no longer makes sense.

9. Remove diagnostic console.logs for tags: [handleResizePointerDown], [outer-edge lookup], [GroupEdgeResize], [startGroupEdgeResize], [useClipDrag], [Marquee].

Hard requirements:
1. Two user-reported symptoms must be fixed: boundary jumps closer to adjacent clip / cuts into other side; doesn't release frames after release.
2. All listed flows must continue to work end-to-end (drag clip in group, drag edges, interior boundary, cross-track, switch to final video, switch to images, gallery drop, auto-save defer, poll-sync defer, projection-shape load migration, shot label menu, per-clip context menu lockdown).
3. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
4. All video-editor tests pass.
5. The 5 deprecated shims and 2 legacy fields physically deleted.
6. No diagnostic console.logs left.

Constraints: scope is video-editor tool only. Follow CLAUDE.md. Prefer Edit over Write. Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Pre-existing failing tests and TS errors in unrelated files left alone. Don't introduce new state management libraries.

Success criteria:
1. Two user-reported symptoms no longer reproduce.
2. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
3. All video-editor tests pass.
4. Deprecated shims and legacy PinnedShotGroup fields deleted.
5. No diagnostic console.logs left.
6. Loading saved file with projection-shape pinnedShotGroups works (migration test passes).
7. Code line count for shot-group-related files decreases net.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):

- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata. - [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-media-duration: timeline media duration: the revised plan still sizes the final-video clip to the shot-group span without any duration source, so shorter final videos can still overrun their real media length. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-signatures: timeline signatures: applying `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` after `materializedata()` would leave `stablesignature` and other config-derived state stale unless the code rebuilds `timelinedata` from the updated config. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-video-duration: timeline-video-duration: the plan routes dropped videos through a new generation path but does not preserve duration metadata in the asset registry. in this repo, inserted video clips fall back to 5 seconds when `registry.duration` is missing, and the existing multi-generation video path explicitly patches duration from drag metadata before dropping. following the plan as written would still create generation-backed videos that are timed incorrectly on the timeline. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Finish the projection-model rollback that a previous megaplan started but only got ~60% done. Currently the data shape is soft-tag (pinnedShotGroups[i] = { shotId, trackId, clipIds }) but several production code paths still operate on the legacy projection-model fields (start, children, offset, duration) behind compatibility shims that are silently lossy. The result: every interaction with a shot group goes through a hybrid where live preview uses soft-tag overrides but commit goes through legacy materialize paths, causing rounding drift, position jumps, stuck preview frames after release, and pointer-capture-loss races.

This is a NARROW, SCOPED finish-the-job megaplan, not a re-architecture. We know exactly what's left.

State of partial completion:

DONE (don't touch unless broken):
- PinnedShotGroup type is soft-tag in src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (with deprecated start? and children? legacy compat fields that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- pinned-group-projection.ts collapsed to ~280 lines: findEnclosingPinnedGroup, categorizeSelection, orderClipIdsByAt (real) + buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups (deprecated compatibility shims that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- useClipDrag.ts rewritten for soft-tag model: uses enclosingGroup.group.clipIds, computes group bounds from live actions, uses orderClipIdsByAt to re-derive clip order after drags
- useClipResize.ts rewritten with resolveGroupMembers, getInteriorNeighbor, computeClipResizeMetaPatch, applyActionBounds helpers — interior-boundary commit branch is in place but routes through applyEdit({type: 'rows'}) which goes through the lossy materializeData path
- useShotGroups.ts rewritten to derive children/start from live row actions
- interaction-state.ts shared interaction lib created with createInteractionState, isInteractionActive, onInteractionEnd, notifyInteractionEndIfIdle
- useTimelinePersistence.ts save scheduler IS gated on interactionStateRef and flushes on gesture end
- usePollSync.ts poll-acceptance gate IS gated on interactionStateRef and re-evaluates on gesture end
- interactionStateRef is plumbed through VideoEditorProvider data context
- TimelineCanvas resize session model collapsed to one discriminated union: 'clip' | 'group-child-boundary' | 'group-edge' with one handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, handleResizePointerMove

NOT DONE (this megaplan):

1. useClipResize.ts interior-boundary commit drift. The interior commit calls applyEdit({type: 'rows', rows: nextRows, metaUpdates}). applyEdit for 'rows' mutations routes through materializeData -> rowsToConfig -> assembleTimelineData -> configToRows. rowsToConfig rounds clip positions to 2 decimal places (0.01s precision) and serializes hold clips as (at, hold) rounded INDEPENDENTLY, which means derived_end != round(actual_end) and tiny gaps can open between adjacent clips after every commit. Fix by increasing rowsToConfig rounding precision to at least 4 decimal places, OR better, make hold clip at/end serialization consistent.

2. usePinnedGroupSync rewrite for soft-tag model in src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts. Currently iterates projection-model children/offset/duration arrays. Rewrite to watch underlying Shot.images[] for added/removed/reordered generation_ids and rebuild clipIds accordingly, deferring during interaction.

3. useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts rewrite for soft-tag model. Currently builds projection-shape nextChildren arrays and passes to buildGroupFromChildren/replaceGroupChildren shims. Rewrite to compute the final-video clip's at/hold directly, update pinnedShotGroups[i].clipIds to [finalVideoClipId], save image clip positions in imageClipSnapshot. Restore reads imageClipSnapshot and restores row actions with updated clipIds.

4. useExternalDrop.ts rewrite for soft-tag shot drop. Compute clip positions directly when adding row actions, create soft-tag group with clipIds.

5. useTimelineTrackManagement.ts moveGroup callsites. Currently uses moveGroup shim which only updates trackId. Rewrite three callsites to also re-derive each clip's row assignment in meta.

6. Migration in migrate.ts repairConfig. Add one-way migration: when loading a saved config with projection-shape groups, convert to soft-tag by extracting clipIds = children.map((c) => c.clipId) and dropping start/children. Add unit test.

7. Delete deprecated compatibility shims from pinned-group-projection.ts: buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups. Also delete from PinnedShotGroup type: deprecated start? and children? fields. Verify type-check clean.

8. Update or delete the 14 failing tests in: serialize.test.ts (1), timeline-save-utils.test.ts (3), useClipDrag.test.tsx (3), useClipEditing.test.ts (6), useShotGroups.test.ts (1). Rewrite to test soft-tag model or delete if invariant no longer makes sense.

9. Remove diagnostic console.logs for tags: [handleResizePointerDown], [outer-edge lookup], [GroupEdgeResize], [startGroupEdgeResize], [useClipDrag], [Marquee].

Hard requirements:
1. Two user-reported symptoms must be fixed: boundary jumps closer to adjacent clip / cuts into other side; doesn't release frames after release.
2. All listed flows must continue to work end-to-end (drag clip in group, drag edges, interior boundary, cross-track, switch to final video, switch to images, gallery drop, auto-save defer, poll-sync defer, projection-shape load migration, shot label menu, per-clip context menu lockdown).
3. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
4. All video-editor tests pass.
5. The 5 deprecated shims and 2 legacy fields physically deleted.
6. No diagnostic console.logs left.

Constraints: scope is video-editor tool only. Follow CLAUDE.md. Prefer Edit over Write. Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Pre-existing failing tests and TS errors in unrelated files left alone. Don't introduce new state management libraries.

Success criteria:
1. Two user-reported symptoms no longer reproduce.
2. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
3. All video-editor tests pass.
4. Deprecated shims and legacy PinnedShotGroup fields deleted.
5. No diagnostic console.logs left.
6. Loading saved file with projection-shape pinnedShotGroups works (migration test passes).
7. Code line count for shot-group-related files decreases net.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. - [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.         Project directory:

        Idea:
Finish the projection-model rollback that a previous megaplan started but only got ~60% done. Currently the data shape is soft-tag (pinnedShotGroups[i] = { shotId, trackId, clipIds }) but several production code paths still operate on the legacy projection-model fields (start, children, offset, duration) behind compatibility shims that are silently lossy. The result: every interaction with a shot group goes through a hybrid where live preview uses soft-tag overrides but commit goes through legacy materialize paths, causing rounding drift, position jumps, stuck preview frames after release, and pointer-capture-loss races.

This is a NARROW, SCOPED finish-the-job megaplan, not a re-architecture. We know exactly what's left.

State of partial completion:

DONE (don't touch unless broken):
- PinnedShotGroup type is soft-tag in src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (with deprecated start? and children? legacy compat fields that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- pinned-group-projection.ts collapsed to ~280 lines: findEnclosingPinnedGroup, categorizeSelection, orderClipIdsByAt (real) + buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups (deprecated compatibility shims that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- useClipDrag.ts rewritten for soft-tag model: uses enclosingGroup.group.clipIds, computes group bounds from live actions, uses orderClipIdsByAt to re-derive clip order after drags
- useClipResize.ts rewritten with resolveGroupMembers, getInteriorNeighbor, computeClipResizeMetaPatch, applyActionBounds helpers — interior-boundary commit branch is in place but routes through applyEdit({type: 'rows'}) which goes through the lossy materializeData path
- useShotGroups.ts rewritten to derive children/start from live row actions
- interaction-state.ts shared interaction lib created with createInteractionState, isInteractionActive, onInteractionEnd, notifyInteractionEndIfIdle
- useTimelinePersistence.ts save scheduler IS gated on interactionStateRef and flushes on gesture end
- usePollSync.ts poll-acceptance gate IS gated on interactionStateRef and re-evaluates on gesture end
- interactionStateRef is plumbed through VideoEditorProvider data context
- TimelineCanvas resize session model collapsed to one discriminated union: 'clip' | 'group-child-boundary' | 'group-edge' with one handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, handleResizePointerMove

NOT DONE (this megaplan):

1. useClipResize.ts interior-boundary commit drift. The interior commit calls applyEdit({type: 'rows', rows: nextRows, metaUpdates}). applyEdit for 'rows' mutations routes through materializeData -> rowsToConfig -> assembleTimelineData -> configToRows. rowsToConfig rounds clip positions to 2 decimal places (0.01s precision) and serializes hold clips as (at, hold) rounded INDEPENDENTLY, which means derived_end != round(actual_end) and tiny gaps can open between adjacent clips after every commit. Fix by increasing rowsToConfig rounding precision to at least 4 decimal places, OR better, make hold clip at/end serialization consistent.

2. usePinnedGroupSync rewrite for soft-tag model in src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts. Currently iterates projection-model children/offset/duration arrays. Rewrite to watch underlying Shot.images[] for added/removed/reordered generation_ids and rebuild clipIds accordingly, deferring during interaction.

3. useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts rewrite for soft-tag model. Currently builds projection-shape nextChildren arrays and passes to buildGroupFromChildren/replaceGroupChildren shims. Rewrite to compute the final-video clip's at/hold directly, update pinnedShotGroups[i].clipIds to [finalVideoClipId], save image clip positions in imageClipSnapshot. Restore reads imageClipSnapshot and restores row actions with updated clipIds.

4. useExternalDrop.ts rewrite for soft-tag shot drop. Compute clip positions directly when adding row actions, create soft-tag group with clipIds.

5. useTimelineTrackManagement.ts moveGroup callsites. Currently uses moveGroup shim which only updates trackId. Rewrite three callsites to also re-derive each clip's row assignment in meta.

6. Migration in migrate.ts repairConfig. Add one-way migration: when loading a saved config with projection-shape groups, convert to soft-tag by extracting clipIds = children.map((c) => c.clipId) and dropping start/children. Add unit test.

7. Delete deprecated compatibility shims from pinned-group-projection.ts: buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups. Also delete from PinnedShotGroup type: deprecated start? and children? fields. Verify type-check clean.

8. Update or delete the 14 failing tests in: serialize.test.ts (1), timeline-save-utils.test.ts (3), useClipDrag.test.tsx (3), useClipEditing.test.ts (6), useShotGroups.test.ts (1). Rewrite to test soft-tag model or delete if invariant no longer makes sense.

9. Remove diagnostic console.logs for tags: [handleResizePointerDown], [outer-edge lookup], [GroupEdgeResize], [startGroupEdgeResize], [useClipDrag], [Marquee].

Hard requirements:
1. Two user-reported symptoms must be fixed: boundary jumps closer to adjacent clip / cuts into other side; doesn't release frames after release.
2. All listed flows must continue to work end-to-end (drag clip in group, drag edges, interior boundary, cross-track, switch to final video, switch to images, gallery drop, auto-save defer, poll-sync defer, projection-shape load migration, shot label menu, per-clip context menu lockdown).
3. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
4. All video-editor tests pass.
5. The 5 deprecated shims and 2 legacy fields physically deleted.
6. No diagnostic console.logs left.

Constraints: scope is video-editor tool only. Follow CLAUDE.md. Prefer Edit over Write. Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Pre-existing failing tests and TS errors in unrelated files left alone. Don't introduce new state management libraries.

Success criteria:
1. Two user-reported symptoms no longer reproduce.
2. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
3. All video-editor tests pass.
4. Deprecated shims and legacy PinnedShotGroup fields deleted.
5. No diagnostic console.logs left.
6. Loading saved file with projection-shape pinnedShotGroups works (migration test passes).
7. Code line count for shot-group-related files decreases net.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] agent-chat-rendering: removing the duplicate assistant turn may hide message_user text if the frontend only renders assistant-role turns as chat bubbles. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] agent-tool-utils: agent-tool-utils: step 6 moves `isrecord` out of `llm/messages.ts` without preserving that module's current export surface for `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, so the refactor plan can break four existing imports unless it adds a re-export or rewires those callers. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the revised grouped-drag plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still leaves `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` stale on cross-track moves. the data model stores `trackid`, and current drag/move helpers only update rows/meta/clip order, so a pinned group dragged to another track would keep pointing at the old track unless the drag commit also patches group metadata. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked step 2.4 against `materializedata()` in `usetimelinecommit` and `assembletimelinedata()` in `timeline-data.ts`. the plan still says to overwrite `pinnedshotgroups` after `materializedata()` returns, but `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so mutating the config afterward would leave signature-derived state stale unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config. Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app

        Idea:
Finish the projection-model rollback that a previous megaplan started but only got ~60% done. Currently the data shape is soft-tag (pinnedShotGroups[i] = { shotId, trackId, clipIds }) but several production code paths still operate on the legacy projection-model fields (start, children, offset, duration) behind compatibility shims that are silently lossy. The result: every interaction with a shot group goes through a hybrid where live preview uses soft-tag overrides but commit goes through legacy materialize paths, causing rounding drift, position jumps, stuck preview frames after release, and pointer-capture-loss races.

This is a NARROW, SCOPED finish-the-job megaplan, not a re-architecture. We know exactly what's left.

State of partial completion:

DONE (don't touch unless broken):
- PinnedShotGroup type is soft-tag in src/tools/video-editor/types/index.ts (with deprecated start? and children? legacy compat fields that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- pinned-group-projection.ts collapsed to ~280 lines: findEnclosingPinnedGroup, categorizeSelection, orderClipIdsByAt (real) + buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups (deprecated compatibility shims that should be DELETED at the end of this refactor)
- useClipDrag.ts rewritten for soft-tag model: uses enclosingGroup.group.clipIds, computes group bounds from live actions, uses orderClipIdsByAt to re-derive clip order after drags
- useClipResize.ts rewritten with resolveGroupMembers, getInteriorNeighbor, computeClipResizeMetaPatch, applyActionBounds helpers — interior-boundary commit branch is in place but routes through applyEdit({type: 'rows'}) which goes through the lossy materializeData path
- useShotGroups.ts rewritten to derive children/start from live row actions
- interaction-state.ts shared interaction lib created with createInteractionState, isInteractionActive, onInteractionEnd, notifyInteractionEndIfIdle
- useTimelinePersistence.ts save scheduler IS gated on interactionStateRef and flushes on gesture end
- usePollSync.ts poll-acceptance gate IS gated on interactionStateRef and re-evaluates on gesture end
- interactionStateRef is plumbed through VideoEditorProvider data context
- TimelineCanvas resize session model collapsed to one discriminated union: 'clip' | 'group-child-boundary' | 'group-edge' with one handleResizePointerDown, updateResize, endResize, handleResizePointerMove

NOT DONE (this megaplan):

1. useClipResize.ts interior-boundary commit drift. The interior commit calls applyEdit({type: 'rows', rows: nextRows, metaUpdates}). applyEdit for 'rows' mutations routes through materializeData -> rowsToConfig -> assembleTimelineData -> configToRows. rowsToConfig rounds clip positions to 2 decimal places (0.01s precision) and serializes hold clips as (at, hold) rounded INDEPENDENTLY, which means derived_end != round(actual_end) and tiny gaps can open between adjacent clips after every commit. Fix by increasing rowsToConfig rounding precision to at least 4 decimal places, OR better, make hold clip at/end serialization consistent.

2. usePinnedGroupSync rewrite for soft-tag model in src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usePinnedShotGroups.ts. Currently iterates projection-model children/offset/duration arrays. Rewrite to watch underlying Shot.images[] for added/removed/reordered generation_ids and rebuild clipIds accordingly, deferring during interaction.

3. useSwitchToFinalVideo.ts rewrite for soft-tag model. Currently builds projection-shape nextChildren arrays and passes to buildGroupFromChildren/replaceGroupChildren shims. Rewrite to compute the final-video clip's at/hold directly, update pinnedShotGroups[i].clipIds to [finalVideoClipId], save image clip positions in imageClipSnapshot. Restore reads imageClipSnapshot and restores row actions with updated clipIds.

4. useExternalDrop.ts rewrite for soft-tag shot drop. Compute clip positions directly when adding row actions, create soft-tag group with clipIds.

5. useTimelineTrackManagement.ts moveGroup callsites. Currently uses moveGroup shim which only updates trackId. Rewrite three callsites to also re-derive each clip's row assignment in meta.

6. Migration in migrate.ts repairConfig. Add one-way migration: when loading a saved config with projection-shape groups, convert to soft-tag by extracting clipIds = children.map((c) => c.clipId) and dropping start/children. Add unit test.

7. Delete deprecated compatibility shims from pinned-group-projection.ts: buildGroupFromChildren, buildGroupFromConfigClips, replaceGroupChildren, moveGroup, projectPinnedShotGroups. Also delete from PinnedShotGroup type: deprecated start? and children? fields. Verify type-check clean.

8. Update or delete the 14 failing tests in: serialize.test.ts (1), timeline-save-utils.test.ts (3), useClipDrag.test.tsx (3), useClipEditing.test.ts (6), useShotGroups.test.ts (1). Rewrite to test soft-tag model or delete if invariant no longer makes sense.

9. Remove diagnostic console.logs for tags: [handleResizePointerDown], [outer-edge lookup], [GroupEdgeResize], [startGroupEdgeResize], [useClipDrag], [Marquee].

Hard requirements:
1. Two user-reported symptoms must be fixed: boundary jumps closer to adjacent clip / cuts into other side; doesn't release frames after release.
2. All listed flows must continue to work end-to-end (drag clip in group, drag edges, interior boundary, cross-track, switch to final video, switch to images, gallery drop, auto-save defer, poll-sync defer, projection-shape load migration, shot label menu, per-clip context menu lockdown).
3. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
4. All video-editor tests pass.
5. The 5 deprecated shims and 2 legacy fields physically deleted.
6. No diagnostic console.logs left.

Constraints: scope is video-editor tool only. Follow CLAUDE.md. Prefer Edit over Write. Don't bundle unrelated cleanups. Pre-existing failing tests and TS errors in unrelated files left alone. Don't introduce new state management libraries.

Success criteria:
1. Two user-reported symptoms no longer reproduce.
2. tsc --noEmit clean for video-editor tree.
3. All video-editor tests pass.
4. Deprecated shims and legacy PinnedShotGroup fields deleted.
5. No diagnostic console.logs left.
6. Loading saved file with projection-shape pinnedShotGroups works (migration test passes).
7. Code line count for shot-group-related files decreases net.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):

- [DEBT] agent-chat-undo-integration: agent chat edits arrive via query invalidation/realtime polling and will bypass the undo stack because those paths are marked skiphistory. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)

(flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the new `useaudioparam()` sketch against `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileeffect.tsx:1-2`. the plan defines it in terms of `interpolate(audio[binding.source], ...)` but does not explicitly import `interpolate` into the new hook file or simplify it to straight arithmetic, so the helper's implementation contract is still looser than it needs to be. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] shot-group-badge-scope: shot-group badge scope: step 3 suppresses the reminder at shot level, but this repo can render multiple shot groups for the same shot, so switching one group to the final video would hide the badge on other groups of that shot too. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] task-deduplication: server-generated idempotency keys would break retry deduplication because retried requests get fresh keys. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: task reference matching: reusing `taskreferencesgeneration` as the only matcher will miss active video-generation tasks for source clips. the helper does not inspect `input_image_generation_ids`, `start_image_generation_id`, or `end_image_generation_id`, even though this repo stores and later reads those fields to identify which timeline generations a travel task is using. that means spinners would fail to appear for at least some travel/video tasks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] ta[REDACTED_SK]: create_generation_task is underspecified against the actual task family system. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-media-duration: timeline media duration: the revised plan still sizes the final-video clip to the shot-group span without any duration source, so shorter final videos can still overrun their real media length. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-shot-group-cleanup: timeline shot-group cleanup: the plan only updates `useshotgroups.test.ts`, but the repository also has direct test coverage for the removed `resolvefinalvideoshotids()` helper and the `timelinecanvas` suggested-group/finalvideoshotids api. without explicitly updating `timelineeditor.test.ts` and `timelinecanvas.test.tsx`, the plan leaves the supporting test surface behind and the post-change suite will fail on deleted symbols and obsolete expectations. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-signatures: timeline signatures: applying `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` after `materializedata()` would leave `stablesignature` and other config-derived state stale unless the code rebuilds `timelinedata` from the updated config. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] timeline-video-duration: timeline-video-duration: the plan routes dropped videos through a new generation path but does not preserve duration metadata in the asset registry. in this repo, inserted video clips fall back to 5 seconds when `registry.duration` is missing, and the existing multi-generation video path explicitly patches duration from drag metadata before dropping. following the plan as written would still create generation-backed videos that are timed incorrectly on the timeline. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] tool-error-propagation: a blanket catch in executetool would silently downgrade db/persistence failures into ordinary tool results, changing the session error/http 500 behavior. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-accessibility: replacing arrow buttons with drag-only handle removes keyboard-accessible reorder; plan only specifies pointersensor. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] track-reorder-testing: no automated tests specified for same-kind boundary rules or save/load persistence of reordered tracks. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] undo-reconciliation: undo reconciliation: step 16's post-commit `reconcilepinnedgroups` would create extra save/history events for a single delete unless it is explicitly coalesced with the triggering edit or marked to skip history. (flagged 2 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] usederivedtimeline: duplicate of correctness-1 — usememo dep array lint violation in sample code (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] video-editor-effect-preview: preview fps source not exposed via existing context (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

- [DEBT] video-editor-effect-registry: o(n) re-renders during effect initialization (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] video-editor-effect-registry: same batching gap as performance-1 (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
